{"parentUuid":"44e92621-b303-462c-8258-d2e307f1f615","isSidechain":false,"attachment":{"type":"mcp_instructions_delta","addedNames":["plugin:context7:context7","plugin:serena:serena"],"addedBlocks":["## plugin:context7:context7\nUse this server to fetch current documentation whenever the user asks about a library, framework, SDK, API, CLI tool, or cloud service -- even well-known ones like React, Next.js, Prisma, Express, Tailwind, Django, or Spring Boot. This includes API syntax, configuration, version migration, library-specific debugging, setup instructions, and CLI tool usage. Use even when you think you know the answer -- your training data may not reflect recent changes. Prefer this over web search for library docs.\n\nDo not use for: refactoring, writing scripts from scratch, debugging business logic, code review, or general programming concepts.","## plugin:serena:serena\nCRITICAL: Before starting to work on a coding task, call the `initial_instructions` tool to read the 'Serena Instructions Manual'."],"removedNames":[]},"type":"attachment","uuid":"27a178d3-4f2c-4be7-856a-fd891c4a994c","timestamp":"2026-05-06T11:42:49.528Z","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"C:\\htdocs\\stockforge","sessionId":"7ce29fc0-b039-4a2a-9e33-a8948d63e223","version":"2.1.124","gitBranch":"main"}
{"parentUuid":"27a178d3-4f2c-4be7-856a-fd891c4a994c","isSidechain":false,"attachment":{"type":"skill_listing","content":"- update-config: Use this skill to configure the Claude Code harness via settings.json. Automated behaviors (\"from now on when X\", \"each time X\", \"whenever X\", \"before/after X\") require hooks configured in settings.json - the harness executes these, not Claude, so memory/preferences cannot fulfill them. Also use for: permissions (\"allow X\", \"add permission\", \"move permission to\"), env vars (\"set X=Y\"), hook troubleshooting, or any changes to settings.json/settings.local.json files. Examples: \"allow npm commands\", \"add bq permission to global settings\", \"move permission to user settings\", \"set DEBUG=true\", \"when claude stops show X\". For simple settings like theme/model, suggest the /config command.\n- keybindings-help: Use when the user wants to customize keyboard shortcuts, rebind keys, add chord bindings, or modify ~/.claude/keybindings.json. Examples: \"rebind ctrl+s\", \"add a chord shortcut\", \"change the submit key\", \"customize keybindings\".\n- simplify: Review changed code for reuse, quality, and efficiency, then fix any issues found.\n- fewer-permission-prompts: Scan your transcripts for common read-only Bash and MCP tool calls, then add a prioritized allowlist to project .claude/settings.json to reduce permission prompts.\n- loop: Run a prompt or slash command on a recurring interval (e.g. /loop 5m /foo). Omit the interval to let the model self-pace. - When the user wants to set up a recurring task, poll for status, or run something repeatedly on an interval (e.g. \"check the deploy every 5 minutes\", \"keep running /babysit-prs\"). Do NOT invoke for one-off tasks.\n- schedule: Create, update, list, or run scheduled remote agents (routines) that execute on a cron schedule. - When the user wants to schedule a recurring remote agent, set up automated tasks, create a cron job for Claude Code, or manage their scheduled agents/routines. Also use when the user wants a one-time scheduled run (\"run this once at 3pm\", \"remind me to check X tomorrow\").\n- claude-api: Build, debug, and optimize Claude API / Anthropic SDK apps. Apps built with this skill should include prompt caching. Also handles migrating existing Claude API code between Claude model versions (4.5 → 4.6, 4.6 → 4.7, retired-model replacements).\nTRIGGER when: code imports `anthropic`/`@anthropic-ai/sdk`; user asks for the Claude API, Anthropic SDK, or Managed Agents; user adds/modifies/tunes a Claude feature (caching, thinking, compaction, tool use, batch, files, citations, memory) or model (Opus/Sonnet/Haiku) in a file; questions about prompt caching / cache hit rate in an Anthropic SDK project.\nSKIP: file imports `openai`/other-provider SDK, filename like `*-openai.py`/`*-generic.py`, provider-neutral code, general programming/ML.\n- ccs-delegation: Auto-activate CCS CLI delegation for deterministic tasks. Parses user input, auto-selects optimal profile (glm/kimi/custom) from ~/.ccs/config.json, enhances prompts with context, executes via `ccs {profile} -p \"task\"` or `ccs {profile}:continue`, and reports results. Triggers on \"use ccs [task]\" patterns, typo/test/refactor keywords. Excludes complex architecture, security-critical code, performance optimization, breaking changes.\n- attach: Port the Claude Code harness layer from this project to a new project directory. Use when bootstrapping a fresh Claude Code project on the same machine, when peers want to copy the harness, or when verifying layer separation via dry-run. Reads .claude/manifest.yaml; copies harness-tagged artifacts only; excludes stockforge biz + personal layers; generates skeleton CLAUDE.md + manifest at target.\n- crawler-reliability: Build reliable web crawlers for data gathering. Use when implementing scrapers for CafeF, NDH, VietnamBiz, YouTube (yt-dlp), Facebook fanpages, or any external data source. Covers Python/Playwright patterns, retry/backoff, selector robustness, rate limiting, monitoring.\n- ddd-tactical-patterns: Apply DDD tactical patterns (aggregate, entity, value object, repository, domain event, anti-corruption layer) correctly in Python/dataclasses code. Use when designing or implementing domain models, repositories, or cross-bounded-context communication in StockForge's 9 BCs.\n- decompose-work: Split a non-trivial task into deterministic-vs-LLM portions before execution. Use when planning multi-step work where some sub-tasks are mechanical (file ops, regex, count, schema validation) and others require judgment (synthesis, design, ambiguity resolution). Outputs structured plan that maps each portion to the cheapest competent tool. Pairs with capability-map.md (model × effort × task_class grounding) and try-n-approaches (S9 — Karpathy autoresearch loop).\n- empirical-probe-first: Probe ALL viable strategies in a multi-option ladder BEFORE committing to one. Use when a session-plan / decision-ADR / spec presents ≥3 strategy options (e.g. \"Strategy A1/A2/A3\" or \"alternative B/C/D\") and the recommended option's source-evidence may be stale. Outputs a probe matrix per strategy + commit decision with empirical citation. Pairs with `vendor-api-probe.sh` (deterministic detection layer; SessionStart hook) and `decompose-work` (deterministic-vs-LLM split per probe). Charter promotion target — codifies L-S32-1 doctrine surfaced at S32 Track A pivot (master-plan recommended A2 vnstock alternate; empirical probe rejected A1+A2; A3 SSI direct httpx adopted instead).\n- evidence-extraction: Extract structured claims from unstructured sources with citation integrity. Use when building extraction pipelines, parsing news articles or KOL transcripts, or anywhere claims are created from raw data. Ensures every claim has source_url, extracted_at, confidence, and verified attribution. Enforces no-LLM-math invariant (I-S1).\n- fastapi-module: Implement FastAPI routers following StockForge conventions. Use when creating a new HTTP endpoint, adding routes, wiring dependency injection, configuring Pydantic DTOs. Covers router boundaries, provider patterns, testing setup. Phase 2+ only — Phase 1 uses Streamlit dashboard directly.\n- grill-maximization: Doctrine for bundling Q&A questions to the human at maximum density per turn (15-20 target, max 25), so each human-in-the-loop touchpoint is information-rich. Use when composing a Q&A bundle for human-workspace/q-and-a/pending/, or when deciding whether to ask one question or wait and bundle. Built on the principle that human-in-the-loop time is the scarcest resource in autonomous coding.\n- hook-diagnostics: Inspect hook event lifecycle (state machine + transcript cache + aggregator output) when triaging failed/stuck hooks or auditing self-awareness rollups. Use during sandwich-verifier review, drift investigation, or post-mortem when component-telemetry.jsonl + dispatch.jsonl + sessions-rollup.tsv need cross-checking.\n- obsidian-vault: Search, create, and manage notes in the StockForge Obsidian vault with wikilinks, frontmatter, and index maintenance. Use when adding content to the knowledge base, navigating entities/concepts, or maintaining the raw/wiki separation. Adapted from mattpocock/skills, MIT licensed. Extended with raw/wiki pattern from Karpathy LLM Wiki.\n- postgres-pgvector: Design Postgres schemas with pgvector and TimescaleDB for StockForge. Use when creating migrations, designing tables, writing queries. Covers vector embeddings for semantic search on Vietnamese text, time-series for price/sentiment data, JSONB vs structured columns.\n- promote-rule: Cluster agent-notes.md entries by lexical similarity, propose promotion of recurring rules to deterministic hook (priority 1), skill (priority 2), or charter (priority 3). Use when agent-notes has accumulated ≥10 new rules since last promotion run, or on-demand at phase boundary. Outputs proposal markdown to observations/. Pairs with capability-map.md (writes new task_class entries) and decompose-work (consumes promotion outcomes).\n- prompt-engineering: Design and optimize LLM prompts for StockForge pipeline stages. Use when writing or refining prompts for claim extraction, KOL recommendation extraction, sentiment analysis, thesis synthesis. Covers context assembly, prompt caching strategy, budget-aware prompts, structured output. Enforces no-LLM-math constraint.\n- qa-escalation: File-based Q&A protocol — composes bundle file in human-workspace/q-and-a/pending/, tracks lifecycle (pending → answered → stale → processed), creates URGENT notification when needed. Use when intent classifier recommends OPEN_QA_BUNDLE / ESCALATE_HUMAN, or grill-maximization has a bundle ready to write. Pairs with grill-maximization (composes content) + Track 5 hooks (auto-move stale).\n- session-memory-l0-l1: Extract structured memories (L0 quick metadata + L1 deep) from a Claude Code session JSONL transcript. L0 is pure regex (file paths, commands, errors, failures incl. Vietnamese phrases, next step). L1 ships the prompt builder + response parser + 15+35 head/tail windowing — caller dispatches the LLM (no auto-invoke in Phase 0). Plus cleanText harness-stripper + TranscriptCache (mtime+size dual-check + LRU). Use when ingesting a session for memory promotion, computing what files/commands/failures the session touched, or preparing an offline LLM extraction.\n- spec-dual-layer: Write specifications with dual-layer structure (business narrative + agent contract). Use when authoring new specs, refining existing specs, or understanding the spec template. Ensures both human stakeholder needs and agent execution needs are served.\n- spec-to-wiki: Convert raw specifications to Obsidian wiki format with wikilinks, frontmatter, and index updates. Use when adding a new spec to the knowledge base, or when regenerating wiki view after spec changes. Maintains raw as source of truth, wiki as navigable view.\n- sync-pull: On-demand confidence-score lookup before a SCOPE+/ARCH/IMPL decision. Reads agent-workspace/memory/sync-tracker/state.tsv + weights.yaml; surfaces the relevant category's score, tier, must-grill flag, and the decision-class threshold. Output drives \"self-decide vs grill\" choice. Use whenever an agent is about to commit a non-trivial decision and needs to ground confidence in historical hit rate per Charter Principle 8 (Calibration over confidence).\n- test-pyramid-balance: Balance unit, integration, and E2E tests properly in Python/pytest. Use when deciding what test to write, reviewing test coverage, or rebalancing skewed test suite. Covers test pyramid shape, BDD vs unit tests, when to use what. Includes stock-domain-specific test categories.\n- try-n-approaches: Karpathy autoresearch outer-loop framing — given a task or signal, generate ≥3 distinct approaches (DEEPEN / BROADEN / ABANDON families), pick one per cheap-first ordering, attach a deterministic metric function (per L-S12-1), and write the experiment frame to learning-data/loop/. Use when an open question surfaces from drift / dogfood / sync-grilling and you need to commit a measurable next step. Pairs with decompose-work (deterministic-vs-LLM split per approach) + capability-map (model × effort grounding) + promote-rule (consumes outcomes).\n- ubiquitous-language: Extract a DDD-style ubiquitous language glossary from the current conversation or domain area. Use when starting work on a new bounded context, when new domain terms emerge, or when verifying consistency between business language and code. Adapted from mattpocock/skills, MIT licensed.\n- user-prompt-intake: Hybrid intent classifier — main session lite-detects trivial prompts, dispatches intent-classifier subagent for complex ones. Use whenever a new user prompt lands (chat or file in human-workspace/user_prompt/) and you need to route the work. Built for full-autonomous loop where every prompt must be classified before action.\n- write-a-skill: Create new Claude Code skills with proper structure, progressive disclosure, and discoverability. Use when extracting a pattern from multiple sessions into a reusable skill, or when user asks to formalize a technique. {"parentUuid":"0d2062aa-aaa9-4df7-a0f6-b78a96b9ce00","isSidechain":false,"attachment":{"type":"nested_memory","path":"C:\\htdocs\\stockforge\\agent-workspace\\CLAUDE.md","content":{"path":"C:\\htdocs\\stockforge\\agent-workspace\\CLAUDE.md","type":"Project","content":"# agent-workspace/ — Contract\n\n> **Audience**: agents working inside `agent-workspace/`.\n> **Established**: Decision 002 § Track 1 (Workspace Dualism Foundation).\n\n## Identity\n\n`agent-workspace/` is the **agent-owned execution + memory** layer. Everything here represents **agent state, decisions, plans, observations, learning**. Agents WRITE here freely (within constitution rules); humans READ here but normally do not edit.\n\nThis separation exists to prevent two failure modes from orch:\n1. **Charter drift via shared-workspace mutation** (orch CF-DOGFOOD-2)\n2. **Human cognitive overload from dense session logs** (UP02 §1.3 — \"human đối mặt hàng chục spec/document quá tải nếu không sắp xếp khoa học\")\n\nThe agent owns this directory but must keep it AUDITABLE — every artifact provenance-linked, every decision sourced.\n\n---\n\n## Subdirectories\n\n| Path | Purpose | Lifecycle |\n|---|---|---|\n| `constitution/` | **IMMUTABLE without explicit user approval**. Charter-tier rules (autonomous-protocol, model-routing, identity-scope, etc.). Track 7 produces drafts in `proposals/`; user explicit approve to move here. | Edit denied by .claude/settings.json; Write only via human approval gate |\n| `memory/project.md` | High-level project state; phase tracker; ADRs (last 5). Update on phase boundaries + architectural decisions. | Long-lived; trim to most recent decisions |\n| `memory/current-execution.md` | **THE single routing source of truth**. Active phase + active session + autonomous_mode flag + routing table. Read FIRST every session. | Long-lived; updated each session boundary |\n| `memory/agent-notes.md` | Learned rules from real experience (post-mortem / drift / user correction). Append-only mostly. | Long-lived |\n| `memory/decisions/` | Sequential ADRs (`NNN-<slug>.md`) + `_template.md` + `README.md` index. 12-field schema (Track 2). | Append-only; supersession via status field |\n| `memory/sessions/` | One file per session: `YYYY-MM-DD-session-N.md`. What happened, decisions made, files touched. | Append-only |\n| `memory/observations/` | Subagent return artifacts (per Track 6 spec). Each subagent writes one observation file describing what it did. | Append; cleaned periodically by aggregator |\n| `memory/checkpoints/` | Session handoff state for self-reboot (Track 5). `latest.md` is canonical pointer; timestamped historical files retained. | Append + latest.md updated each checkpoint |\n| `memory/patterns-discovered/` | Pattern mining outputs (Track 0 — DONE) + ongoing pattern catalog. | Append; significant patterns promoted to constitution |\n| `memory/drift-logs/` | Drift-check (DR1-DR12) results; runs on hook + on-demand. | Time-series append |\n| `memory/post-mortems/` | After significant failure or thesis-revoked event: what failed, root cause, prevention rule. | Append |\n| `memory/thesis-log/` | Stock-domain thesis exploration entries. Read-only for IMPL sessions. | Append + revisited per calibration |\n| `memory/sync-tracker/` | Track 8a output: Confidence Score SQLite + weights.yaml + auto-rendered `_index.md`. | Live updated by hooks |\n| `memory/self-awareness/` | Track 9 output: model × effort × thinking profile cards + known-issues + best-practices. | Live updated by Stop-hook aggregator |\n| `memory/mistake-log.md` | Track 7 deliverable: structured failure catalog. What went wrong / Root cause / Prevention rule / Severity. | Append; pre-flight read by all agents |\n| `session-plans/pending/` | Plans authored, awaiting execution. Phase 0 plan lives here. | Move to `completed/` when done |\n| `session-plans/completed/` | Executed plans; archived for reference + retrospective input. | Append |\n| `quality-reports/{deterministic,probabilistic,drift-reports}/` | Tier-1/2/3 gate outputs (CLAUDE.md § Quality Gates). | Per-run files |\n| `ubiquitous-language/` | DDD glossary + domain-mapping + drift-log. Stockforge stock-domain vocabulary. | Append; canonical via `/drill-me` |\n| `calibration/` | Empirical hit-rate data per signal type. Confidence claims MUST trace here (CLAUDE.md hard rule). | Append per thesis post-mortem |\n| `research/` | Research notes from `research-scanner` subagent dispatches (e.g., reference-repo studies). | Append |\n\n---\n\n## Contract Rules (BINDING)\n\n1. **Constitution is immutable absent explicit human approval.** `Edit/Write` to `agent-workspace/constitution/**` is in `.claude/settings.json` deny list. Track 7 writes constitution drafts to `proposals/`; user explicit approve moves them.\n\n2. **`memory/decisions/` is append-mostly + supersession-status.** Don't delete decisions; mark `status: superseded-by-D-NNN` to revoke. Sequential numbering (`001`, `002`, ...) — don't reuse numbers.\n\n3. **`current-execution.md` is the routing source-of-truth.** No skill, agent, or command may hardcode phase paths or session paths from memory; resolve via this file. Stale paths in CLAUDE.md or skills = anti-pattern.\n\n4. **Never edit `obsidian-vault/raw/`.** Immutable. Vault writes only to `wiki/`. (Established in stockforge CLAUDE.md.)\n\n5. **Provenance is mandatory.** Every decision file references its source (human prompt / spec clause / drift detection / agent inference). Every thesis cites source + as-of date (I-S2). Every confidence claim cites historical hit rate (I-S20-equivalent).\n\n6. **Agents never `git commit` in this directory unless user explicitly requests.** Stage only.\n\n7. **`patterns-discovered/` artifacts are READ-ONLY for production code.** Patterns must be promoted to constitution / skills / hook scripts before becoming binding. (Otherwise they're informal notes, not contracts.)\n\n---\n\n## Reading Priority for Agent\n\nPer `agent-workspace/constitution/autonomous-protocol.md` (Track 7) and stockforge `CLAUDE.md` § Session Protocol:\n\n1. **`memory/current-execution.md`** — first; resolve active track\n2. **`memory/project.md`** — project state\n3. **`memory/checkpoints/latest.md`** if a recent checkpoint exists (within last 24h) — resume context\n4. **`memory/agent-notes.md`** — learned rules\n5. **`memory/mistake-log.md`** — pre-flight failure catalog\n6. **Last 3 files in `memory/sessions/`** — recent context\n7. **`session-plans/pending/<active-plan>.md`** — current plan\n8. **Relevant constitution files** as task demands (don't load all every session)\n9. **Relevant skills + agents + commands** as task demands\n\n---\n\n## Anti-Patterns to Avoid\n\n(From `memory/patterns-discovered/SYNTHESIS.md` § 6, full catalog 23 items. Highest-priority for this directory:)\n\n- **AP-1 Same-agent self-review** — never review your own implementation; dispatch fresh-context verifier\n- **AP-2 Self-track wind-down** — `.transcript-tokens` real-transcript is authoritative, not LLM self-track\n- **AP-5 Charter-coherence defer overriding user-CRITICAL** — re-read all `user_prompt/*` at every phase entry; never silently defer USER-CRITICAL\n- **AP-7 Performative SC ticking** — defer with explicit prerequisites > fire vacuous proposals\n- **AP-8 Pre-staged work causing checkpoint drift** — update `current-execution.md` immediately on task complete, not session-end\n- **AP-17 Identity drift** — stockforge is AI-first VN stock advisory; not generic financial framework (see `constitution/identity-scope.md` once Track 7 ships)\n- **AP-23 Continuous LLM-Guardian** — deterministic hooks = Guardian; LLM Guardian only at session-end aggregation\n\n---\n\n## Connection to human-workspace/\n\nThis directory is the agent's domain. Human's domain is `human-workspace/`. The two communicate through:\n- Human → Agent: `human-workspace/user_prompt/`, `human-workspace/decisions/`\n- Agent → Human: `human-workspace/notifications/`, `human-workspace/q-and-a/pending/`\n- Bidirectional: `human-workspace/q-and-a/answered/` — agent writes pending; either human or agent (per Auto-mv rule below) moves resolved bundles to answered/\n- Audit trail: this directory's `memory/decisions/` references `human-workspace/` source files via path pointers\n\n**Auto-mv rule (HH-E.2 — D-031 ratification, 2026-05-05)**: Agent MAY mv a bundle from `q-and-a/pending/` to `q-and-a/answered/` IFF ALL of the following hold:\n\n1. **Frontmatter signal**: bundle frontmatter `status:` field value starts with one of: `answered-`, `closed-`, `resolved-`. Examples already in repo: `answered-via-chat`, `answered-via-AskUserQuestion`, `answered-2026-05-04-via-chat`. Detection is deterministic (head -20 of file + grep `^status:`).\n2. **No human-veto signal**: bundle frontmatter has NO `wait_until:` ISO-8601 timestamp greater than current epoch — if present, agent MUST defer mv until that timestamp passes. Allows human to override auto-mv per-bundle without contract amendment.\n3. **No global pause**: file `human-workspace/q-and-a/.auto-mv-paused` does NOT exist — global kill switch for the auto-mv mechanism (single empty file presence pauses all auto-mv).\n4. **Hook validation**: the mv is performed by `scripts/hooks/qa-pending-auto-mover.sh` (Stop hook). Direct manual `mv` invocation by agent (e.g. via Bash tool) is STILL forbidden — only the validated hook path is authorized.\n\nAgent never writes to `human-workspace/` outside the designated `q-and-a/pending/` write channel + the auto-mv rule above + `notifications/` write channel (existing).\n","contentDiffersFromDisk":false},"displayPath":"agent-workspace\\CLAUDE.md"},"type":"attachment","uuid":"48f09357-b92c-4d3a-9b8f-6c3df8b8e5e2","timestamp":"2026-05-06T11:43:02.854Z","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"C:\\htdocs\\stockforge","sessionId":"7ce29fc0-b039-4a2a-9e33-a8948d63e223","version":"2.1.124","gitBranch":"main"}

{"parentUuid":"2cf1049c-0550-4235-83a1-616c0758b9e8","isSidechain":false,"attachment":{"type":"nested_memory","path":"C:\\htdocs\\stockforge\\human-workspace\\CLAUDE.md","content":{"path":"C:\\htdocs\\stockforge\\human-workspace\\CLAUDE.md","type":"Project","content":"# human-workspace/ — Contract\n\n> **Audience**: agents reading anything inside `human-workspace/`.\n> **Established**: Decision 002 § Track 1 (Workspace Dualism Foundation).\n\n## Identity\n\n`human-workspace/` is the **human-authored authority** layer. Everything here represents **human decisions, scope, intent, and verification**. Agents READ this directory as authoritative; agents do NOT WRITE here except in one designated channel (`q-and-a/pending/` — where agents post questions for human to answer).\n\nThis separation exists because of a failure mode observed in the orch sister project (orch `decisions/040`, post-mortem CF-DOGFOOD-2): when human and agent share a single workspace without contract, agent silently overrides human-CRITICAL items via technical-defer logic. The user explicitly raised this concern in `human-workspace/user_prompt/20260429_02_init.txt` §1.1.\n\n---\n\n## Subdirectories\n\n| Path | Purpose | Who writes |\n|---|---|---|\n| `user_prompt/` | Human's mid-flight scope/idea drops. Naming: `YYYYMMDD_NN_<slug>.txt`. Files are immutable post-creation (audit trail). | **Human only**. Agent: read-only. |\n| `decisions/` | Human's strategic decisions (e.g., scope-lock, charter-revision-trigger, peer-share authorization). Format: `D-H-<NNN>-<slug>.md`. | **Human only**. Agent: read-only. |\n| `q-and-a/pending/` | **The ONE channel where agent writes inside human-workspace**. Agent drops Q&A bundles per Grill Maximization doctrine; human responds inline or moves to `answered/`. | **Both** (agent writes new bundles; human writes answers). |\n| `q-and-a/answered/` | Q&A bundles human has answered (with answers inline). Agent reads answers and acts. Archive forever; provenance trail. | **Human moves** (drag from `pending/`); agent reads. |\n| `q-and-a/stale/` | Pending bundles that exceeded their `expected_answer_by` (default 24h, urgent 4h). Auto-moved by SessionStart hook (Track 5). | **Hook moves**; both read. |\n| `notifications/` | Agent push to human — summaries, alerts, milestones. Format: `N-<TS>-<level>-<slug>.md`. Levels: INFO / SUMMARY / ALERT. | **Agent writes**; human reads. |\n\n---\n\n## Contract Rules (BINDING)\n\n1. **Agent NEVER writes to `user_prompt/` or `decisions/`.** Permission deny. If something feels like a human decision but the human hasn't written it, dispatch a Q&A bundle into `q-and-a/pending/` instead.\n\n2. **`user_prompt/` files are immutable post-creation.** Agent never deletes, renames, or edits these files. They are the audit trail of human interventions.\n\n3. **Q&A bundle format** (per Track 4 spec): YAML frontmatter (`id`, `topic`, `opened_at`, `expected_answer_by`, `priority`, `related_decisions`, `status`) + question body + answer-fill section. Each bundle = up to 25 questions per Grill Maximization doctrine; split if more.\n\n4. **Agent does not move files between `q-and-a/{pending,answered,stale}/`** — that's human's role (or hook's, in case of stale). Agent only WRITES new pending bundles and READS answers.\n\n5. **Notifications are read-only for human.** Human shouldn't edit `notifications/*` files; if disagreement with notification content, drop a `user_prompt/` instead. Notifications are the agent's account of events.\n\n6. **Provenance pointers**: every `agent-workspace/memory/decisions/D-<NNN>` should reference its source `human-workspace/user_prompt/<file>` or `human-workspace/decisions/D-H-<NNN>` if the agent's decision derives from human input.\n\n7. **No multi-tenancy in this directory.** Per identity-scope.md (Track 7), stockforge is single-tenant Phase 0-5; if peer share happens it's via git-fork, each peer has their own `human-workspace/` per fork.\n\n---\n\n## Reading Priority for Agent\n\nWhen loading session context (per `agent-workspace/constitution/autonomous-protocol.md` Track 7):\n\n1. **All `user_prompt/*.txt`** — every prompt, not just most recent. Per orch lesson (decision-040 USER-CRITICAL tier): never silently defer human-CRITICAL items.\n2. **All `decisions/*.md`** — human's strategic decisions are above charter principles in priority; charter is below human (charter was authored by human in the first place).\n3. **Latest `q-and-a/answered/*`** — recent answers update agent's understanding.\n4. **Open `q-and-a/pending/*`** — flag if waiting on human answers.\n5. **Recent `notifications/*`** (last 7 days) — for situational awareness.\n\n---\n\n## Agent Self-Check\n\nBefore writing to `human-workspace/q-and-a/pending/`, ask:\n- Is this question genuinely something the human must decide? (Charter principle / Charter-affecting / SCOPE-affecting / above ARCH-tier confidence threshold)\n- Have I already grilled this in a recent bundle? (Don't ask twice; don't pollute pending/)\n- Am I bundling per Grill Maximization (target 15-20 questions, max 25)? Or splintering across many small bundles?\n\nIf the answer to any is \"I'm not sure\" — DON'T write the bundle. Either answer it yourself with charter-principle reasoning + log decision, or wait until ambiguity clears.\n","contentDiffersFromDisk":false},"displayPath":"human-workspace\\CLAUDE.md"},"type":"attachment","uuid":"404cd723-cd68-4fde-999a-b1971ab96c32","timestamp":"2026-05-06T11:43:44.013Z","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"C:\\htdocs\\stockforge","sessionId":"7ce29fc0-b039-4a2a-9e33-a8948d63e223","version":"2.1.124","gitBranch":"main"}

First version is WARN-ONLY (does not\n6\t# auto-archive — manual archive is safer; agent triages next turn per Q-E2 promotion-priority\n7\t# rule).\n8\t#\n9\t# Wires: Stop hook in .claude/settings.json. Soft-warn only (exit 0 always; never blocks).\n10\t# Output: per-violation lines to .session-hooks.log + 1 notification when ≥1 violation.\n11\t#\n12\t# Cap thresholds (per Q-RCA-1 = A acceptance):\n13\t#   - current-execution.md: ≤ 5 active session rows (## S<N> headers); ≤ 200 LOC total\n14\t#   - agent-notes.md: ≤ 700 LOC total (charter rules ~485 + digest ~50 + safety margin)\n15\t#   - mistake-log.md: ≤ 200 LOC total\n16\t#   - component-telemetry.jsonl: ≤ 10 MB (rotated weekly by telemetry-rotate.sh — separate hook)\n17\t#\n18\t# Per L-S10-1: split-local; if/then/fi only.\n19\t# Per L-S80-1: empirical regex pre-test passed.\n20\t# Per L-S80-2: no `grep -c ... || echo 0` multi-line capture trap.\n21\t# Per L-S69-1: artifact-verifier whitelist — this hook is a verifier; main agent-notes/mistake-log/current-execution\n22\t#   are its targets; no self-reference.\n23\t\n24\tset -uo pipefail\n25\ttrap 'exit 0' ERR\n26\t\n27\tPROJECT_DIR=\"${CLAUDE_PROJECT_DIR:-.}\"\n28\tMEM_DIR=\"$PROJECT_DIR/agent-workspace/memory\"\n29\tLOG=\"$MEM_DIR/.session-hooks.log\"\n30\tNOTIF_DIR=\"$PROJECT_DIR/human-workspace/notifications\"\n31\tTS=\"$(date -Iseconds 2>/dev/null || echo unknown)\"\n32\t\n33\tmkdir -p \"$(dirname \"$LOG\")\" 2>/dev/null || true\n34\t\n35\tVIOLATIONS=0\n36\tVIOLATION_LINES=\"\"\n37\t\n38\t# Cap 1: current-execution.md ≤ 5 session rows + ≤ 200 LOC\n39\tCE_FILE=\"$MEM_DIR/current-execution.md\"\n40\tif [ -f \"$CE_FILE\" ]; then\n41\t  CE_LOC=$(wc -l < \"$CE_FILE\" 2>/dev/null)\n42\t  CE_LOC=\"${CE_LOC:-0}\"\n43\t  CE_SESSIONS=$(grep -c \"^## S[0-9]\" \"$CE_FILE\" 2>/dev/null)\n44\t  CE_SESSIONS=\"${CE_SESSIONS:-0}\"\n45\t  if [ \"$CE_LOC\" -gt 200 ]; then\n46\t    VIOLATIONS=$((VIOLATIONS + 1))\n47\t    VIOLATION_LINES=\"$VIOLATION_LINES\n48\t[$TS] tracking-retention: current-execution.md LOC=$CE_LOC > cap 200 (suggest archive oldest sessions to dated archive file)\"\n49\t  fi\n50\t  if [ \"$CE_SESSIONS\" -gt 5 ]; then\n51\t    VIOLATIONS=$((VIOLATIONS + 1))\n52\t    VIOLATION_LINES=\"$VIOLATION_LINES\n53\t[$TS] tracking-retention: current-execution.md sessions=$CE_SESSIONS > cap 5 (archive oldest sessions to current-execution-archive-YYYY-MM-DD-S<from>-to-S<to>.md)\"\n54\t  fi\n55\tfi\n56\t\n57\t# Cap 2: agent-notes.md ≤ 700 LOC; full lesson bodies (### L-S) should NOT be inline (digest only)\n58\tAN_FILE=\"$MEM_DIR/agent-notes.md\"\n59\tif [ -f \"$AN_FILE\" ]; then\n60\t  AN_LOC=$(wc -l < \"$AN_FILE\" 2>/dev/null)\n61\t  AN_LOC=\"${AN_LOC:-0}\"\n62\t  AN_LESSONS=$(grep -c \"^### L-S\" \"$AN_FILE\" 2>/dev/null)\n63\t  AN_LESSONS=\"${AN_LESSONS:-0}\"\n64\t  if [ \"$AN_LOC\" -gt 700 ]; then\n65\t    VIOLATIONS=$((VIOLATIONS + 1))\n66\t    VIOLATION_LINES=\"$VIOLATION_LINES\n67\t[$TS] tracking-retention: agent-notes.md LOC=$AN_LOC > cap 700 (digest section grew; consider sub-archive)\"\n68\t  fi\n69\t  # If digest-only policy held, ### L-S header count = 0 in main file (digest uses table rows, not ### headers)\n70\t  if [ \"$AN_LESSONS\" -gt 0 ]; then\n104\t  TS=\"$(date -u +%FT%TZ 2>/dev/null || true)\"\n105\t  mkdir -p \"$NOTIF_DIR\" 2>/dev/null || true\n106\t  NOTIF_FILE=\"$NOTIF_DIR/${TS//[:.]/-}-checkpoint-mentions-incomplete.md\"\n107\t  {\n108\t    printf '# Checkpoint mentions incomplete (L-S67-5 / M-S67-3 prevention)\\n\\n'\n109\t    printf '**Detected at**: %s (Stop hook)\\n' \"$TS\"\n110\t    printf '**Checkpoint**: `agent-workspace/memory/checkpoints/latest.md`\\n'\n111\t    printf '**Checkpoint mtime > session start**: %s > %s\\n\\n' \"$CKPT_MTIME\" \"$SESSION_START_TS\"\n112\t    printf '## Git-status entries NOT mentioned by basename in checkpoint (after whitelist filter)\\n\\n'\n113\t    printf '%b' \"$UNMENTIONED\" | awk 'NF>0 {print \"- `\"$0\"`\"}'\n114\t    printf '\\n## Recommended action\\n\\n'\n115\t    printf 'Per L-S67-5: agent must run `git status --short` BEFORE authoring checkpoint\\n'\n116\t    printf '\"what survived\" narrative. M-S67-3 was: S67 checkpoint claimed only\n# D1 survived, but git status showed D2+D3+D5 also in working tree → narrative drift\n# from empirical state. This Stop hook detects post-hoc: if checkpoints/latest.md was\n# modified this session AND git status shows entries whose basenames are NOT mentioned\n# in the checkpoint text → emit WARN + notification (after whitelist filter).\n#\n# Wired: Stop (after autonomous-stop-watchdog.sh, before bash-hook-lint.sh)\n# Kill switch: STOCKFORGE_CHECKPOINT_VERIFIER_DISABLE=1\n# Detection signal: checkpoint mtime > .session-ready mtime (session-start-bootstrap.sh\n# writes .session-ready on every SessionStart per its line ~124).\n# Per L-S58-1 ERR-trap-aware: if/then/fi not [ x ] && Y\n# Per L-S11-1 portability: bash + awk + grep + sed only\n\nset -uo pipefail\ntrap 'exit 0' ERR\n\nPROJECT_DIR=\"${CLAUDE_PROJECT_DIR:-.}\"\nMEMORY_DIR=\"$PROJECT_DIR/agent-workspace/memory\"\nCKPT=\"$MEMORY_DIR/checkpoints/latest.md\"\nSESSION_READY=\"$MEMORY_DIR/.session-ready\"\nNOTIF_DIR=\"$PROJECT_DIR/human-workspace/notifications\"\n\n# Kill switch\nif [ \"${STOCKFORGE_CHECKPOINT_VERIFIER_DISABLE:-0}\" = \"1\" ]; then\n  exit 0\nfi\n\n# Only fire on Stop event\nPAYLOAD=\"$(cat 2>/dev/null || true)\"\nHOOK_EVENT=\"\"\nif [ -n \"$PAYLOAD\" ]; then\n  HOOK_EVENT=\"$(printf '%s' \"$PAYLOAD\" | grep -o '\"hook_event_name\":\"[^\"]*\"' 2>/dev/null | head -1 | sed 's/\"hook_event_name\":\"//;s/\"//' || true)\"\nfi\nif [ \"$HOOK_EVENT\" != \"Stop\" ]; then\n  exit 0\nfi\n\n# Skip if no checkpoint or no session marker\nif [ ! -f \"$CKPT\" ]; then\n  exit 0\nfi\nif [ ! -f \"$SESSION_READY\" ]; then\n  exit 0\nfi\n\n# Compare mtimes (Linux stat -c%Y / macOS stat -f%m fallback)\nCKPT_MTIME=\"$(stat -c%Y \"$CKPT\" 2>/dev/null || stat -f%m \"$CKPT\" 2>/dev/null || echo 0)\"\nSESSION_START_TS=\"$(stat -c%Y \"$SESSION_READY\" 2>/dev/null || stat -f%m \"$SESSION_READY\" 2>/dev/null || echo 0)\"\n\n# If checkpoint not modified this session, skip\nif [ \"$CKPT_MTIME\" -le \"$SESSION_START_TS\" ]; then\n  exit 0\nfi\n\n# Run git status --short in PROJECT_DIR\ncd \"$PROJECT_DIR\" 2>/dev/null || exit 0\nGIT_STATUS=\"$(git status --short 2>/dev/null || true)\"\nif [ -z \"$GIT_STATUS\" ]; then\n  exit 0\nfi\n\n# Whitelist: auto-generated/transient paths NOT expected in checkpoint narrative.\n# Tested against working-tree paths from `git status --short` output (after status-code prefix strip).\n# Configurable via env STOCKFORGE_CHECKPOINT_VERIFIER_EXTRA_WHITELIST (extra ERE patterns appended).\nWHITELIST_PATTERN='^(agent-workspace/memory/(indexes/|\\.|etl-queue/|\\.precompact-snapshots/|checkpoints/latest\\.md|component-telemetry\\.jsonl|cost-ledger\\.tsv|dispatch\\.jsonl|\\.continue-injector|\\.last-event-ts|\\.session-|\\.qa-last-scan|\\.transcript-tokens|\\.attestation-checked-|\\.dispatch-pending|\\.profile-template-fired|\\.mode-d-recovery-fired|\\.unattested-observations\\.tsv|sync-state\\.md|sync-tracker/_index\\.md|self-awareness/(sessions-rollup\\.tsv|profiles/)|drift-logs/[0-9]{4}-[0-9]{2}-[0-9]{2}-rollup\\.md|boot-summary\\.md|attestation-log\\.tsv)|agent-workspace/learning-data/|agent-workspace/raw-sessions/|human-workspace/notifications/|.*\\.tmp$)'\nif [ -n \"${STOCKFORGE_CHECKPOINT_VERIFIER_EXTRA_WHITELIST:-}\" ]; then\n  WHITELIST_PATTERN=\"${WHITELIST_PATTERN}|${STOCKFORGE_CHECKPOINT_VERIFIER_EXTRA_WHITELIST}\"\nfi\n\nUNMENTIONED=\"\"\nUNMENTIONED_COUNT=0\n\n# Iterate git status entries; skip whitelisted; check basename in checkpoint\nwhile IFS= read -r line; do\n  if [ -z \"$line\" ]; then\n    continue\n  fi\n  # `git status --short` output: 2-char status code + space + path (or rename has \" -> \")\n  # Strip first 3 chars to get path\n  path=\"${line:3}\"\n  # Handle rename: \"old -> new\" — take new path\n  if printf '%s' \"$path\" | grep -q ' -> '; then\n    path=\"$(printf '%s' \"$path\" | sed 's/.* -> //')\"\n  fi\n  if [ -z \"$path\" ]; then\n    continue\n  fi\n  # Whitelist check\n  if printf '%s' \"$path\" | grep -qE \"$WHITELIST_PATTERN\" 2>/dev/null; then\n    continue\n  fi\n  bn=\"$(basename \"$path\")\"\n  # Look for basename literal in checkpoint text\n  if ! grep -qF \"$bn\" \"$CKPT\" 2>/dev/null; then\n    UNMENTIONED_COUNT=$((UNMENTIONED_COUNT + 1))\n    UNMENTIONED=\"${UNMENTIONED}${path}\\n\"\n  fi\ndone <<< \"$GIT_STATUS\"\n\nif [ \"$UNMENTIONED_COUNT\" -ge 1 ]; then\n  TS=\"$(date -u +%FT%TZ 2>/dev/null || true)\"\n  mkdir -p \"$NOTIF_DIR\" 2>/dev/null || true\n  NOTIF_FILE=\"$NOTIF_DIR/${TS//[:.]/-}-checkpoint-mentions-incomplete.md\"\n  {\n    printf '# Checkpoint mentions incomplete (L-S67-5 / M-S67-3 prevention)\\n\\n'\n    printf '**Detected at**: %s (Stop hook)\\n' \"$TS\"\n    printf '**Checkpoint**: `agent-workspace/memory/checkpoints/latest.md`\\n'\n    printf '**Checkpoint mtime > session start**: %s > %s\\n\\n' \"$CKPT_MTIME\" \"$SESSION_START_TS\"\n    printf '## Git-status entries NOT mentioned by basename in checkpoint (after whitelist filter)\\n\\n'\n    printf '%b' \"$UNMENTIONED\" | awk 'NF>0 {print \"- `\"$0\"`\"}'\n    printf '\\n## Recommended action\\n\\n'\n    printf 'Per L-S67-5: agent must run `git status --short` BEFORE authoring checkpoint\\n'\n    printf '\"what survived\" narrative. The hook found entries above that exist in working tree\\n'\n    printf 'but do NOT appear by basename in the checkpoint text.\\n\\n'\n    printf '1. Re-read git status; cross-check intentional vs missed\\n'\n    printf '2. If intentional omission → ignore (false positive — consider extending whitelist via\\n'\n    printf '   `STOCKFORGE_CHECKPOINT_VERIFIER_EXTRA_WHITELIST` env var)\\n'\n    printf '3. If missed → amend checkpoint to reflect actual state (M-S67-3 recurrence pattern)\\n'\n  } > \"$NOTIF_FILE\"\n  printf 'WARN: %d unmentioned git-status entry(ies) in checkpoint — see %s\\n' \\\n    \"$UNMENTIONED_COUNT\" \"$NOTIF_FILE\" >&2\nfi\n\nexit 0\n","numLines":128,"startLine":1,"totalLines":128}},"sourceToolAssistantUUID":"63888d69-0f5d-4170-a323-bbd14852e310","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"C:\\htdocs\\stockforge","sessionId":"7ce29fc0-b039-4a2a-9e33-a8948d63e223","version":"2.1.124","gitBranch":"main"}

{"parentUuid":"b60573a0-d60b-4473-972d-3639581e3587","isSidechain":false,"promptId":"bb8d886f-775e-4cb7-8ed6-e61ec9c43337","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01TwTAF6nDBTXYiL1iYTFXvP","type":"tool_result","content":"The file C:\\htdocs\\stockforge\\scripts\\hooks\\tracking-retention.sh has been updated successfully."}]},"uuid":"5f3b1b76-bb6c-4ef6-ad04-7fafae4d1dd3","timestamp":"2026-05-06T11:46:27.751Z","toolUseResult":{"filePath":"C:\\htdocs\\stockforge\\scripts\\hooks\\tracking-retention.sh","oldString":"  CE_LOC=$(wc -l < \"$CE_FILE\" 2>/dev/null)\n  CE_LOC=\"${CE_LOC:-0}\"\n  CE_SESSIONS=$(grep -c \"^## S[0-9]\" \"$CE_FILE\" 2>/dev/null)\n  CE_SESSIONS=\"${CE_SESSIONS:-0}\"","newString":"  CE_LOC=$(wc -l < \"$CE_FILE\" 2>/dev/null || echo 0)\n  CE_LOC=\"${CE_LOC:-0}\"\n  CE_SESSIONS=$(grep -c \"^## S[0-9]\" \"$CE_FILE\" 2>/dev/null || echo 0)\n  CE_SESSIONS=\"${CE_SESSIONS:-0}\"","originalFile":"#!/usr/bin/env bash\n# tracking-retention.sh — Stop-hook retention guard for tracking files (S99 RCA Layer 1)\n#\n# Purpose: warn (and emit notification) when tracking files exceed their cap thresholds.\n# Per RCA-2026-05-06-S98 user pick Q-RCA-1 = A. First version is WARN-ONLY (does not\n# auto-archive — manual archive is safer; agent triages next turn per Q-E2 promotion-priority\n# rule).\n#\n# Wires: Stop hook in .claude/settings.json. Soft-warn only (exit 0 always; never blocks).\n# Output: per-violation lines to .session-hooks.log + 1 notification when ≥1 violation.\n#\n# Cap thresholds (per Q-RCA-1 = A acceptance):\n#   - current-execution.md: ≤ 5 active session rows (## S<N> headers); ≤ 200 LOC total\n#   - agent-notes.md: ≤ 700 LOC total (charter rules ~485 + digest ~50 + safety margin)\n#   - mistake-log.md: ≤ 200 LOC total\n#   - component-telemetry.jsonl: ≤ 10 MB (rotated weekly by telemetry-rotate.sh — separate hook)\n#\n# Per L-S10-1: split-local; if/then/fi only.\n# Per L-S80-1: empirical regex pre-test passed.\n# Per L-S80-2: no `grep -c ... || echo 0` multi-line capture trap.\n# Per L-S69-1: artifact-verifier whitelist — this hook is a verifier; main agent-notes/mistake-log/current-execution\n#   are its targets; no self-reference.\n\nset -uo pipefail\ntrap 'exit 0' ERR\n\nPROJECT_DIR=\"${CLAUDE_PROJECT_DIR:-.}\"\nMEM_DIR=\"$PROJECT_DIR/agent-workspace/memory\"\nLOG=\"$MEM_DIR/.session-hooks.log\"\nNOTIF_DIR=\"$PROJECT_DIR/human-workspace/notifications\"\nTS=\"$(date -Iseconds 2>/dev/null || echo unknown)\"\n\nmkdir -p \"$(dirname \"$LOG\")\" 2>/dev/null || true\n\nVIOLATIONS=0\nVIOLATION_LINES=\"\"\n\n# Cap 1: current-execution.md ≤ 5 session rows + ≤ 200 LOC\nCE_FILE=\"$MEM_DIR/current-execution.md\"\nif [ -f \"$CE_FILE\" ]; then\n  CE_LOC=$(wc -l < \"$CE_FILE\" 2>/dev/null)\n  CE_LOC=\"${CE_LOC:-0}\"\n  CE_SESSIONS=$(grep -c \"^## S[0-9]\" \"$CE_FILE\" 2>/dev/null)\n  CE_SESSIONS=\"${CE_SESSIONS:-0}\"\n  if [ \"$CE_LOC\" -gt 200 ]; then\n    VIOLATIONS=$((VIOLATIONS + 1))\n    VIOLATION_LINES=\"$VIOLATION_LINES\n[$TS] tracking-retention: current-execution.md LOC=$CE_LOC > cap 200 (suggest archive oldest sessions to dated archive file)\"\n  fi\n  if [ \"$CE_SESSIONS\" -gt 5 ]; then\n    VIOLATIONS=$((VIOLATIONS + 1))\n    VIOLATION_LINES=\"$VIOLATION_LINES\n[$TS] tracking-retention: current-execution.md sessions=$CE_SESSIONS > cap 5 (archive oldest sessions to current-execution-archive-YYYY-MM-DD-S<from>-to-S<to>.md)\"\n  fi\nfi\n\n# Cap 2: agent-notes.md ≤ 700 LOC; full lesson bodies (### L-S) should NOT be inline (digest only)\nAN_FILE=\"$MEM_DIR/agent-notes.md\"\nif [ -f \"$AN_FILE\" ]; then\n  AN_LOC=$(wc -l < \"$AN_FILE\" 2>/dev/null)\n  AN_LOC=\"${AN_LOC:-0}\"\n  AN_LESSONS=$(grep -c \"^### L-S\" \"$AN_FILE\" 2>/dev/null)\n  AN_LESSONS=\"${AN_LESSONS:-0}\"\n  if [ \"$AN_LOC\" -gt 700 ]; then\n    VIOLATIONS=$((VIOLATIONS + 1))\n    VIOLATION_LINES=\"$VIOLATION_LINES\n[$TS] tracking-retention: agent-notes.md LOC=$AN_LOC > cap 700 (digest section grew; consider sub-archive)\"\n  fi\n  # If digest-only policy held, ### L-S header count = 0 in main file (digest uses table rows, not ### headers)\n  if [ \"$AN_LESSONS\" -gt 0 ]; then\n    VIOLATIONS=$((VIOLATIONS + 1))\n    VIOLATION_LINES=\"$VIOLATION_LINES\n[$TS] tracking-retention: agent-notes.md has $AN_LESSONS full ### L-S lesson bodies inline (cap policy = digest only; archive bodies to agent-notes-archive-YYYY-MM-DD.md)\"\n  fi\nfi\n\n# Cap 3: mistake-log.md ≤ 200 LOC; full ### M-S bodies should NOT be inline (digest only)\nML_FILE=\"$MEM_DIR/mistake-log.md\"\nif [ -f \"$ML_FILE\" ]; First version is WARN-ONLY (does not\n# auto-archive — manual archive is safer; agent triages next turn per Q-E2 promotion-priority\n# rule).\n#\n# Wires: Stop hook in .claude/settings.json. Soft-warn only (exit 0 always; never blocks).\n# Output: per-violation lines to .session-hooks.log + 1 notification when ≥1 violation.\n#\n# Cap thresholds (per Q-RCA-1 = A acceptance):\n#   - current-execution.md: ≤ 5 active session rows (## S<N> headers); ≤ 200 LOC total\n#   - agent-notes.md: ≤ 700 LOC total (charter rules ~485 + digest ~50 + safety margin)\n#   - mistake-log.md: ≤ 200 LOC total\n#   - component-telemetry.jsonl: ≤ 10 MB (rotated weekly by telemetry-rotate.sh — separate hook)\n#\n# Per L-S10-1: split-local; if/then/fi only.\n# Per L-S80-1: empirical regex pre-test passed.\n# Per L-S80-2: no `grep -c ... || echo 0` multi-line capture trap.\n# Per L-S69-1: artifact-verifier whitelist — this hook is a verifier; main agent-notes/mistake-log/current-execution\n#   are its targets; no self-reference.\n\nset -uo pipefail\ntrap 'exit 0' ERR\n\nPROJECT_DIR=\"${CLAUDE_PROJECT_DIR:-.}\"\nMEM_DIR=\"$PROJECT_DIR/agent-workspace/memory\"\nLOG=\"$MEM_DIR/.session-hooks.log\"\nNOTIF_DIR=\"$PROJECT_DIR/human-workspace/notifications\"\nTS=\"$(date -Iseconds 2>/dev/null || echo unknown)\"\n\nmkdir -p \"$(dirname \"$LOG\")\" 2>/dev/null || true\n\nVIOLATIONS=0\nVIOLATION_LINES=\"\"\n\n# Cap 1: current-execution.md ≤ 5 session rows + ≤ 200 LOC\nCE_FILE=\"$MEM_DIR/current-execution.md\"\nif [ -f \"$CE_FILE\" ]; then\n  CE_LOC=$(wc -l < \"$CE_FILE\" 2>/dev/null || echo 0)\n  CE_LOC=\"${CE_LOC:-0}\"\n  CE_SESSIONS=$(grep -c \"^## S[0-9]\" \"$CE_FILE\" 2>/dev/null || echo 0)\n  CE_SESSIONS=\"${CE_SESSIONS:-0}\"\n  if [ \"$CE_LOC\" -gt 200 ]; then\n    VIOLATIONS=$((VIOLATIONS + 1))\n    VIOLATION_LINES=\"$VIOLATION_LINES\n[$TS] tracking-retention: current-execution.md LOC=$CE_LOC > cap 200 (suggest archive oldest sessions to dated archive file)\"\n  fi\n  if [ \"$CE_SESSIONS\" -gt 5 ]; then\n    VIOLATIONS=$((VIOLATIONS + 1))\n    VIOLATION_LINES=\"$VIOLATION_LINES\n[$TS] tracking-retention: current-execution.md sessions=$CE_SESSIONS > cap 5 (archive oldest sessions to current-execution-archive-YYYY-MM-DD-S<from>-to-S<to>.md)\"\n  fi\nfi\n\n# Cap 2: agent-notes.md ≤ 700 LOC; full lesson bodies (### L-S) should NOT be inline (digest only)\nAN_FILE=\"$MEM_DIR/agent-notes.md\"\nif [ -f \"$AN_FILE\" ]; then\n  AN_LOC=$(wc -l < \"$AN_FILE\" 2>/dev/null || echo 0)\n  AN_LOC=\"${AN_LOC:-0}\"\n  AN_LESSONS=$(grep -c \"^### L-S\" \"$AN_FILE\" 2>/dev/null || echo 0)\n  AN_LESSONS=\"${AN_LESSONS:-0}\"\n  if [ \"$AN_LOC\" -gt 700 ]; then\n    VIOLATIONS=$((VIOLATIONS + 1))\n    VIOLATION_LINES=\"$VIOLATION_LINES\n[$TS] tracking-retention: agent-notes.md LOC=$AN_LOC > cap 700 (digest section grew; consider sub-archive)\"\n  fi\n  # If digest-only policy held, ### L-S header count = 0 in main file (digest uses table rows, not ### headers)\n  if [ \"$AN_LESSONS\" -gt 0 ]; then\n    VIOLATIONS=$((VIOLATIONS + 1))\n    VIOLATION_LINES=\"$VIOLATION_LINES\n[$TS] tracking-retention: agent-notes.md has $AN_LESSONS full ### L-S lesson bodies inline (cap policy = digest only; archive bodies to agent-notes-archive-YYYY-MM-DD.md)\"\n  fi\nfi\n\n# Cap 3: mistake-log.md ≤ 200 LOC; full ### M-S bodies should NOT be inline (digest only)\nML_FILE=\"$MEM_DIR/mistake-log.md\"\nif [ -f \"$ML_FILE\" ]; then\n  ML_LOC=$(wc -l < \"$ML_FILE\" 2>/dev/null || echo 0)\n  ML_LOC=\"${ML_LOC:-0}\"\n  ML_MISTAKES=$(grep -c \"^### M-S\" \"$ML_FILE\" 2>/dev/null || echo 0)\n  ML_MISTAKES=\"${ML_MISTAKES:-0}\"\n  if [ \"$ML_LOC\" -gt 200 ]; then\n    VIOLATIONS=$((VIOLATIONS + 1))\n    VIOLATION_LINES=\"$VIOLATION_LINES\n[$TS] tracking-retention: mistake-log.md LOC=$ML_LOC > cap 200 (digest grew; archive bodies)\"\n  fi\n  if [ \"$ML_MISTAKES\" -gt 0 ]; then\n    VIOLATIONS=$((VIOLATIONS + 1))\n    VIOLATION_LINES=\"$VIOLATION_LINES\n[$TS] tracking-retention: mistake-log.md has $ML_MISTAKES full ### M-S mistake bodies inline (cap policy = digest only; archive to mistake-log-archive-YYYY-MM-DD.md)\"\n  fi\nfi\n\n# Cap 4: component-telemetry.jsonl ≤ 10 MB (weekly rotate handled by telemetry-rotate.sh)\nTEL_FILE=\"$MEM_DIR/component-telemetry.jsonl\"\nif [ -f \"$TEL_FILE\" ]; then\n  TEL_BYTES=$(stat -c %s \"$TEL_FILE\" 2>/dev/null || stat -f %z \"$TEL_FILE\" 2>/dev/null)\n  TEL_BYTES=\"${TEL_BYTES:-0}\"\n  if [ \"$TEL_BYTES\" -gt 10485760 ]; then\n    VIOLATIONS=$((VIOLATIONS + 1))\n    VIOLATION_LINES=\"$VIOLATION_LINES\n[$TS] tracking-retention: component-telemetry.jsonl size=$TEL_BYTES bytes > cap 10485760 (10 MB); ensure telemetry-rotate.sh weekly is scheduled\"\n  fi\nfi\n\n# Log\nif [ \"$VIOLATIONS\" -gt 0 ]; then\n  printf '%s' \"$VIOLATION_LINES\" >> \"$LOG\" 2>/dev/null\n  printf '\\n' >> \"$LOG\" 2>/dev/null\nfi\n\n# Notification (single file, overwrite-style; latest only)\nif [ \"$VIOLATIONS\" -gt 0 ]; then\n  mkdir -p \"$NOTIF_DIR\" 2>/dev/null || true\n  NOTIF_FILE=\"$NOTIF_DIR/tracking-retention-cap-breach.md\"\n  {\n    printf '# Tracking Retention Cap Breach — %s\\n\\n' \"$TS\"\n    printf 'Per RCA-2026-05-06-S98 Layer 1 retention rule, %d violation(s) detected:\\n' \"$VIOLATIONS\"\n    printf '%s\\n\\n' \"$VIOLATION_LINES\"\n    printf 'Manual archive playbook: see CLAUDE.md § Tracking retention. Auto-archive deferred for safety (Q-RCA-1 = A first version).\\n'\n  } > \"$NOTIF_FILE\" 2>/dev/null || true\nfi\n\n# Summary line for diagnostics\nprintf '[%s] tracking-retention: violations=%d ce_loc=%s ce_sessions=%s an_loc=%s an_lessons=%s ml_loc=%s ml_mistakes=%s tel_bytes=%s\\n' \\\n  \"$TS\" \"$VIOLATIONS\" \"${CE_LOC:-na}\" \"${CE_SESSIONS:-na}\" \"${AN_LOC:-na}\" \"${AN_LESSONS:-na}\" \"${ML_LOC:-na}\" \"${ML_MISTAKES:-na}\" \"${TEL_BYTES:-na}\" >> \"$LOG\" 2>/dev/null\n\nexit 0\n","structuredPatch":[{"oldStart":38,"oldLines":9,"newStart":38,"newLines":9,"lines":[" # Cap 1: current-execution.md ≤ 5 session rows + ≤ 200 LOC"," CE_FILE=\"$MEM_DIR/current-execution.md\""," if [ -f \"$CE_FILE\" ]; then","-  CE_LOC=$(wc -l < \"$CE_FILE\" 2>/dev/null || echo 0)","+  CE_LOC=$(wc -l < \"$CE_FILE\" 2>/dev/null || true)","   CE_LOC=\"${CE_LOC:-0}\"","-  CE_SESSIONS=$(grep -c \"^## S[0-9]\" \"$CE_FILE\" 2>/dev/null || echo 0)","+  CE_SESSIONS=$(grep -c \"^## S[0-9]\" \"$CE_FILE\" 2>/dev/null || true)","   CE_SESSIONS=\"${CE_SESSIONS:-0}\"","   if [ \"$CE_LOC\" -gt 200 ]; then","     VIOLATIONS=$((VIOLATIONS + 1))"]}],"userModified":false,"replaceAll":false},"sourceToolAssistantUUID":"1a8fda92-6fb9-478c-b455-ce42ec92a5bb","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"C:\\htdocs\\stockforge","sessionId":"7ce29fc0-b039-4a2a-9e33-a8948d63e223","version":"2.1.124","gitBranch":"main"}

successfully."}]},"uuid":"28d52cd1-da23-43eb-a85c-b460813fc70e","timestamp":"2026-05-06T11:51:46.773Z","toolUseResult":{"filePath":"C:\\htdocs\\stockforge\\scripts\\hooks\\pre-checkpoint-close-verifier.sh","oldString":"WHITELIST_PATTERN='^(agent-workspace/memory/(indexes/|\\.|etl-queue/|\\.precompact-snapshots/|checkpoints/latest\\.md|component-telemetry\\.jsonl|cost-ledger\\.tsv|dispatch\\.jsonl|\\.continue-injector|\\.last-event-ts|\\.session-|\\.qa-last-scan|\\.transcript-tokens|\\.attestation-checked-|\\.dispatch-pending|\\.profile-template-fired|\\.mode-d-recovery-fired|\\.unattested-observations\\.tsv|sync-state\\.md|sync-tracker/_index\\.md|self-awareness/(sessions-rollup\\.tsv|profiles/)|drift-logs/[0-9]{4}-[0-9]{2}-[0-9]{2}-rollup\\.md|boot-summary\\.md|attestation-log\\.tsv)|agent-workspace/learning-data/|agent-workspace/raw-sessions/|human-workspace/notifications/|.*\\.tmp$)'","newString":"WHITELIST_PATTERN='^(agent-workspace/memory/(indexes/|\\.|etl-queue/|\\.precompact-snapshots/|checkpoints/latest\\.md|component-telemetry\\.jsonl|cost-ledger\\.tsv|dispatch\\.jsonl|\\.continue-injector|\\.last-event-ts|\\.session-|\\.qa-last-scan|\\.transcript-tokens|\\.attestation-checked-|\\.dispatch-pending|\\.profile-template-fired|\\.mode-d-recovery-fired|\\.unattested-observations\\.tsv|sync-state\\.md|sync-tracker/_index\\.md|self-awareness/(sessions-rollup\\.tsv|profiles/)|drift-logs/[0-9]{4}-[0-9]{2}-[0-9]{2}-rollup\\.md|boot-summary\\.md|attestation-log\\.tsv|sessions/|observations/|telemetry-archive/|(agent-notes|mistake-log|current-execution)-archive-[0-9]{4}-[0-9]{2}-[0-9]{2}.*\\.md)|agent-workspace/learning-data/|agent-workspace/raw-sessions/|agent-workspace/session-plans/completed/|human-workspace/notifications/|.*\\.tmp$)'","originalFile":"#!/usr/bin/env bash\n# pre-checkpoint-close-verifier.sh — Stop hook detecting M-S67-3 pattern.\n# L-S67-5 mitigation: agent should run `git status --short` BEFORE authoring \"what\n# survived\" narrative in checkpoint close. M-S67-3 was: S67 checkpoint claimed only\n# D1 survived, but git status showed D2+D3+D5 also in working tree → narrative drift\n# from empirical state. This Stop hook detects post-hoc: if checkpoints/latest.md was\n# modified this session AND git status shows entries whose basenames are NOT mentioned\n# in the checkpoint text → emit WARN + notification (after whitelist filter).\n#\n# Wired: Stop (after autonomous-stop-watchdog.sh, before bash-hook-lint.sh)\n# Kill switch: STOCKFORGE_CHECKPOINT_VERIFIER_DISABLE=1\n# Detection signal: checkpoint mtime > .session-ready mtime (session-start-bootstrap.sh\n# writes .session-ready on every SessionStart per its line ~124).\n# Per L-S58-1 ERR-trap-aware: if/then/fi not [ x ] && Y\n# Per L-S11-1 portability: bash + awk + grep + sed only\n\nset -uo pipefail\ntrap 'exit 0' ERR\n\nPROJECT_DIR=\"${CLAUDE_PROJECT_DIR:-.}\"\nMEMORY_DIR=\"$PROJECT_DIR/agent-workspace/memory\"\nCKPT=\"$MEMORY_DIR/checkpoints/latest.md\"\nSESSION_READY=\"$MEMORY_DIR/.session-ready\"\nNOTIF_DIR=\"$PROJECT_DIR/human-workspace/notifications\"\n\n# Kill switch\nif [ \"${STOCKFORGE_CHECKPOINT_VERIFIER_DISABLE:-0}\" = \"1\" ]; then\n  exit 0\nfi\n\n# Only fire on Stop event\nPAYLOAD=\"$(cat 2>/dev/null || true)\"\nHOOK_EVENT=\"\"\nif [ -n \"$PAYLOAD\" ]; then\n  HOOK_EVENT=\"$(printf '%s' \"$PAYLOAD\" | grep -o '\"hook_event_name\":\"[^\"]*\"' 2>/dev/null | head -1 | sed 's/\"hook_event_name\":\"//;s/\"//' || true)\"\nfi\nif [ \"$HOOK_EVENT\" != \"Stop\" ]; then\n  exit 0\nfi\n\n# Skip if no checkpoint or no session marker\nif [ ! -f \"$CKPT\" ]; then\n  exit 0\nfi\nif [ ! -f \"$SESSION_READY\" ]; then\n  exit 0\nfi\n\n# Compare mtimes (Linux stat -c%Y / macOS stat -f%m fallback)\nCKPT_MTIME=\"$(stat -c%Y \"$CKPT\" 2>/dev/null || stat -f%m \"$CKPT\" 2>/dev/null || echo 0)\"\nSESSION_START_TS=\"$(stat -c%Y \"$SESSION_READY\" 2>/dev/null || stat -f%m \"$SESSION_READY\" 2>/dev/null || echo 0)\"\n\n# If checkpoint not modified this session, skip\nif [ \"$CKPT_MTIME\" -le \"$SESSION_START_TS\" ]; then\n  exit 0\nfi\n\n# Run git status --short in PROJECT_DIR\ncd \"$PROJECT_DIR\" 2>/dev/null || exit 0\nGIT_STATUS=\"$(git status --short 2>/dev/null || true)\"\nif [ -z \"$GIT_STATUS\" ]; then\n  exit 0\nfi\n\n# Whitelist: auto-generated/transient paths NOT expected in checkpoint narrative.\n# Tested against working-tree paths from `git status --short` output (after status-code prefix strip).\n# Configurable via env STOCKFORGE_CHECKPOINT_VERIFIER_EXTRA_WHITELIST (extra ERE patterns appended).\nWHITELIST_PATTERN='^(agent-workspace/memory/(indexes/|\\.|etl-queue/|\\.precompact-snapshots/|checkpoints/latest\\.md|component-telemetry\\.jsonl|cost-ledger\\.tsv|dispatch\\.jsonl|\\.continue-injector|\\.last-event-ts|\\.session-|\\.qa-last-scan|\\.transcript-tokens|\\.attestation-checked-|\\.dispatch-pending|\\.profile-template-fired|\\.mode-d-recovery-fired|\\.unattested-observations\\.tsv|sync-state\\.md|sync-tracker/_index\\.md|self-awareness/(sessions-rollup\\.tsv|profiles/)|drift-logs/[0-9]{4}-[0-9]{2}-[0-9]{2}-rollup\\.md|boot-summary\\.md|attestation-log\\.tsv)|agent-workspace/learning-data/|agent-workspace/raw-sessions/|human-workspace/notifications/|.*\\.tmp$)'\nif [ -n \"${STOCKFORGE_CHECKPOINT_VERIFIER_EXTRA_WHITELIST:-}\" ]; then\n  WHITELIST_PATTERN=\"${WHITELIST_PATTERN}|${STOCKFORGE_CHECKPOINT_VERIFIER_EXTRA_WHITELIST}\"\nfi\n\nUNMENTIONED=\"\"\nUNMENTIONED_COUNT=0\n\n# Iterate git status entries; skip whitelisted; check basename in checkpoint\nwhile IFS= read -r line; do\n  if [ -z \"$line\" ]; then\n    continue\n  fi\n  # `git status --short` output: 2-char status code + space + path (or rename has \" -> \")\n  # Strip first 3 chars to get path\n  path=\"${line:3}\"\n  # Handle rename: \"old -> new\" — take new path\n  if printf '%s' \"$path\" | grep -q ' -> '; then\n    path=\"$(printf '%s' \"$path\" | sed 's/.* -> //')\"\n  fi\n  if [ -z \"$path\" ]; then\n    continue\n  fi\n  # Whitelist check\n  if printf '%s' \"$path\" | grep -qE \"$WHITELIST_PATTERN\" 2>/dev/null; then\n    continue\n  fi\n  bn=\"$(basename \"$path\")\"\n  # Look for basename literal in checkpoint text\n  if ! grep -qF \"$bn\" \"$CKPT\" 2>/dev/null; then\n    UNMENTIONED_COUNT=$((UNMENTIONED_COUNT + 1))\n    UNMENTIONED=\"${UNMENTIONED}${path}\\n\"\n  fi\ndone <<< \"$GIT_STATUS\"\n\nif [ \"$UNMENTIONED_COUNT\" -ge 1 ]; then\n  TS=\"$(date -u +%FT%TZ 2>/dev/null || true)\"\n  mkdir -p \"$NOTIF_DIR\" 2>/dev/null || true\n  NOTIF_FILE=\"$NOTIF_DIR/${TS//[:.]/-}-checkpoint-mentions-incomplete.md\"\n  {\n    printf '# Checkpoint mentions incomplete (L-S67-5 / M-S67-3 prevention)\\n\\n'\n    printf '**Detected at**: %s (Stop hook)\\n' \"$TS\"\n    printf '**Checkpoint**: `agent-workspace/memory/checkpoints/latest.md`\\n'\n    printf '**Checkpoint mtime > session start**: %s > %s\\n\\n' \"$CKPT_MTIME\" \"$SESSION_START_TS\"\n    printf '## Git-status entries NOT mentioned by basename in checkpoint (after whitelist filter)\\n\\n'\n    printf '%b' \"$UNMENTIONED\" | awk 'NF>0 {print \"- `\"$0\"`\"}'\n    printf '\\n## Recommended action\\n\\n'\n    printf 'Per L-S67-5: agent must run `git status --short` BEFORE authoring checkpoint\\n'\n    printf '\"what survived\" narrative. The hook found entries above that exist in working tree\\n'\n    printf 'but do NOT appear by basename in the checkpoint text.\\n\\n'\n    printf '1. Re-read git status; cross-check intentional vs missed\\n'\n    printf '2. If intentional omission → ignore (false positive — consider extending whitelist via\\n'\n    printf '   `STOCKFORGE_CHECKPOINT_VERIFIER_EXTRA_WHITELIST` env var)\\n'\n    printf '3. If missed → amend checkpoint to reflect actual state (M-S67-3 recurrence pattern)\\n'\n  } > \"$NOTIF_FILE\"\n  printf 'WARN: %d unmentioned git-status entry(ies) in checkpoint — see %s\\n' \\\n Rationale:\n115\t# Original design (L-S48-1) gated on `.session-ready` mtime → SessionTag advanced only on\n116\t# SessionStart events (`/clear`, fresh launch, `claude --resume`). Within a single session,\n117\t# SessionTag NEVER changes → first injector fire writes `.continue-fired-{Tag}` → ALL\n118\t# subsequent autonomous Stop→Mode-D→continue-injector handoffs hit \"already fired\" silent\n119\t# exit → autonomous-full loop dead within session boundaries. Smoking gun S53 19:59→20:16:\n120\t# Mode-D fired correctly but injector silent-exited because per-tick marker was carried\n121\t# over from prior bootstrap fire 16min earlier.\n122\t#\n123\t# Replacement contract (post-S53):\n124\t#   - 60s rate-limit (above) handles burst-spam within single SessionStart event\n125\t#   - Each caller has its OWN per-event idempotency marker:\n126\t#     - bootstrap (SessionStart): one fire per SessionStart event (no marker; bootstrap\n127\t#       itself only fires once per SessionStart)\n128\t#     - Mode-A (API truncation): $false\n137\tfor ($i = 1; $i -le $RetryCount; $i++) {\n138\t    Log \"attempt $i/$RetryCount\"\n139\t    $focused = Try-FocusClaudeTerminal\n140\t    if (-not $focused) {\n141\t        Log (\"skip attempt {0}: no verified claude TUI window; will not type into foreground (L-S48-1 prevention)\" -f $i)\n142\t        if ($i -lt $RetryCount) { Start-Sleep -Milliseconds $RetryDelayMs }\n143\t (attempt $i)\"\n152\t        $Sent = $true\n153\t        break\n154\t    } else {\n155\t        Log \"send failed (attempt $i)\"\n156\t    }\n157\t    if ($i -lt $RetryCount) { Start-Sleep prompt and begins autonomous resume loop.\n# Ported from orch v2.2.0 (verbatim).\n\nparam(\n    [int]$InitialDelayMs = 2500,\n    [int]$RetryCount = 3,\n    [int]$RetryDelayMs = 3000,\n    [string]$LogFile = \"\"\n)\n\n$ErrorActionPreference = \"Continue\"\n\nif (-not $LogFile) {\n    $ProjectDir = (Get-Item $PSScriptRoot).Parent.Parent.FullName\n    $LogDir = Join-Path $ProjectDir \"agent-workspace\\memory\\handoff-logs\"\n    New-Item -ItemType Directory -Force -Path $LogDir | Out-Null\n    $LogFile = Join-Path $LogDir (\"continue-injector-{0}.log\" -f (Get-Date -Format \"yyyyMMddTHHmmssZ\"))\n}\nfunction Log($msg) { Add-Content -Path $LogFile -Value (\"[{0}] {1}\" -f (Get-Date -Format \"o\"), $msg) }\n\nLog \"start pid=$PID initialDelay=$InitialDelayMs retries=$RetryCount retryDelay=$RetryDelayMs\"\n\nStart-Sleep -Milliseconds $InitialDelayMs\n\nAdd-Type -AssemblyName System.Windows.Forms\nAdd-Type @\"\nusing System;\nusing System.Runtime.InteropServices;\npublic class Win32 {\n  [DllImport(\"user32.dll\")] public static extern bool SetForegroundWindow(IntPtr hWnd);\n  [DllImport(\"user32.dll\")] public static extern bool ShowWindow(IntPtr hWnd, int nCmdShow);\n  [DllImport(\"user32.dll\")] public static extern IntPtr GetForegroundWindow();\n  [DllImport(\"user32.dll\")] public static extern int GetWindowThreadProcessId(IntPtr hWnd, out int lpdwProcessId);\n}\n\"@\n\nfunction Try-FocusClaudeTerminal {\n    try {\n        $claudes = Get-Process claude -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue\n        if (-not $claudes) { Log \"no claude.exe running\"; return $false }\n\n        foreach ($c in $claudes) {\n            try {\n                $parentId = (Get-CimInstance Win32_Process -Filter \"ProcessId = $($c.Id)\" -ErrorAction Stop).ParentProcessId\n                if (-not $parentId) { continue }\n                $parent = Get-Process -Id $parentId -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue\n                if (-not $parent) { continue }\n                Log (\"claude pid={0} parent={1}({2}) mainWnd=0x{3:X}\" -f $c.Id, $parent.ProcessName, $parent.Id, [int64]$parent.MainWindowHandle)\n                if ($parent.MainWindowHandle -ne [IntPtr]::Zero) {\n                    [Win32]::ShowWindow($parent.MainWindowHandle, 9) | Out-Null\n                    [Win32]::SetForegroundWindow($parent.MainWindowHandle) | Out-Null\n                    Start-Sleep -Milliseconds 400\n                    return $true\n                }\n\n                $cur = $parent\n                $hops = 0\n                while ($cur -and $hops -lt 6) {\n                    $hops++\n                    try {\n                        $curParentId = (Get-CimInstance Win32_Process -Filter \"ProcessId = $($cur.Id)\" -ErrorAction Stop).ParentProcessId\n                    } catch { break }\n                    if (-not $curParentId) { break }\n                    $curParent = Get-Process -Id $curParentId -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue\n                    if (-not $curParent) { break }\n                    Log (\"claude-ancestor hop {0} pid={1} name={2} mainWnd=0x{3:X}\" -f $hops, $curParent.Id, $curParent.ProcessName, [int64]$curParent.MainWindowHandle)\n                    if ($curParent.MainWindowHandle -ne [IntPtr]::Zero) {\n                        [Win32]::ShowWindow($curParent.MainWindowHandle, 9) | Out-Null\n                        [Win32]::SetForegroundWindow($curParent.MainWindowHandle) | Out-Null\n                        Start-Sleep -Milliseconds 400\n                        return $true\n                    }\n                    $cur = $curParent\n                }\n            } catch { Log \"parent-walk error: $_\" }\n        }\n\n        Log \"no claude.exe ancestor has valid window; refusing fallback to random terminal (L-S48-1 bug: prior code grabbed first WindowsTerminal/cmd/bash with valid hwnd, typed continue into [System.Windows.Forms.SendKeys]::SendWait(\"continue\")\n        Start-Sleep -Milliseconds 150\n        [System.Windows.Forms.SendKeys]::SendWait(\"{ENTER}\")\n        return $true\n    } catch { Log \"SendKeys error: $_\"; return $false }\n}\n\n$ProjectDir = (Get-Item $PSScriptRoot).Parent.Parent.FullName\n\n# Hard global rate-limit (covers ALL entry points: bootstrap startup/clear/resume + Mode-A/B/C/D\n# recovery). Prevents bootstrap touching .session-ready on every SessionStart from generating a\n# fresh idempotency tick that lets another injector fire seconds after the previous. Empirical\n# trigger 2026-05-05: 180 injector spawns + 160 SessionStarts in single morning, sustained\n# wrong-window \"continue\" typing into user TUI. See agent-workspace/memory/agent-notes.md L-S48-1.\n$RateLimitMarker = Join-Path $ProjectDir \"agent-workspace\\memory\\.continue-injector-last-fire\"\nif (Test-Path $RateLimitMarker) {\n    $LastFire = (Get-Item $RateLimitMarker -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue).LastWriteTime\n    if ($LastFire) {\n        $AgeSeconds = ((Get-Date) - $LastFire).TotalSeconds\n        if ($AgeSeconds -lt 60) {\n            Log (\"rate-limit: another injector fired {0:N1}s ago (<60s); exiting\" -f $AgeSeconds)\n            exit 0\n        }\n    }\n}\nSet-Content -Path $RateLimitMarker -Value (Get-Date -Format \"o\") -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue\n\n# Per-tick idempotency was REMOVED at S53 (M-S53-3 fix). Rationale:\n# Original design (L-S48-1) gated on `.session-ready` mtime → SessionTag advanced only on\n# SessionStart events (`/clear`, fresh launch, `claude --resume`). Within a single session,\n# SessionTag NEVER changes → first injector fire writes `.continue-fired-{Tag}` → ALL\n# subsequent autonomous Stop→Mode-D→continue-injector handoffs hit \"already fired\" silent\n# exit → autonomous-full loop dead within session boundaries. Smoking gun S53 19:59→20:16:\n# Mode-D fired correctly but injector silent-exited because per-tick marker was carried\n# over from prior bootstrap fire 16min earlier.\n#\n# Replacement contract (post-S53):\n#   - 60s rate-limit (above) handles burst-spam within single SessionStart event\n#   - Each caller has its OWN per-event idempotency marker:\n#     - bootstrap (SessionStart): one fire per SessionStart event (no marker; bootstrap\n#       itself only fires once per SessionStart)\n#     - Mode-A (API truncation): `.api-truncation-recovery-fired-$RECOVERY_KEY`\n#     - Mode-C (premature wind-down): `.mode-c-recovery-fired-$MODE_C_KEY`\n#     - Mode-D (clean handoff): `.mode-d-recovery-fired-$MODE_D_KEY`\n#   - Cross-caller: 60s rate-limit ensures no two callers fire injector within 60s window\n# The L-S48-1 incident scenario (180 spawns + 160 SessionStarts in one morning) is fully\n# covered by the 60s rate-limit since spawns averaged ~30-40 min apart well above 60s.\n$SessionTag = \"post-S53-no-per-tick\"\n\n$Sent = $false\nfor ($i = 1; $i -le $RetryCount; $i++) {\n    Log \"attempt $i/$RetryCount\"\n    $focused = Try-FocusClaudeTerminal\n    if (-not $focused) {\n        Log (\"skip attempt {0}: no verified claude TUI window; will not type into foreground (L-S48-1 prevention)\" -f $i)\n        if ($i -lt $RetryCount) { Start-Sleep -Milliseconds $RetryDelayMs }\n        continue\n    }\n    $fg = [Win32]::GetForegroundWindow()\n    [int]$pid_fg = 0\n    [Win32]::GetWindowThreadProcessId($fg, [ref]$pid_fg) | Out-Null\n    $fgProc = Get-Process -Id $pid_fg -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue\n    Log (\"foreground pid={0} name={1}\" -f $pid_fg, ($fgProc.ProcessName))\n    if (Send-Continue) {\n        Log \"continue+Enter sent (attempt $i)\"\n        $Sent = $true\n        break\n    } else {\n        Log \"send failed (attempt $i)\"\n    }\n    if ($i -lt $RetryCount) { Start-Sleep -Milliseconds $RetryDelayMs }\n}\n\nLog (\"injector done sent={0}\" -f $Sent)\n","numLines":161,"startLine":1,"totalLines":161}},"sourceToolAssistantUUID":"1f1c0155-0f9e-4718-b421-5b4506783de1","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"C:\\htdocs\\stockforge","sessionId":"7ce29fc0-b039-4a2a-9e33-a8948d63e223","version":"2.1.124","gitBranch":"main"}

\"BYPASSED\" log entry; no marker\n#   TC3 — wrong tool_name (Bash) → exit 0; no marker, no log entry\n#   TC4 — Edit + non-checkpoint file_path → exit 0; no marker\n#   TC5 — Edit + checkpoints/latest.md + autonomous_mode=false → marker SET; {"parentUuid":"efef48b6-5e87-4123-bcc3-70a032257b7f","isSidechain":false,"message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-7","id":"msg_01ChzMLF9ZRZCeq5WBQtUHpQ","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_01X6YB4jNJSPWK4WUSkd8SHk","name":"Write","input":{"file_path":"C:\\htdocs\\stockforge\\scripts\\hooks\\firing-tests\\tracking-retention-fire-test.sh","content":"#!/usr/bin/env bash\n# Firing-test for tracking-retention.sh (S99 RCA Layer 1; S100 firing-test backfill per T7 hard rule #2).\n#\n# Hook purpose (Stop hook):\n#   - reads cap thresholds for current-execution.md / agent-notes.md / mistake-log.md / component-telemetry.jsonl\n#   - emits per-violation lines to .session-hooks.log + 1 notification when ≥1 violation\n#   - exit 0 always (soft-warn; never blocks)\n#\n# Test cases:\n#   TC1 — all files missing → 0 violations, summary line emitted, no notification\n#   TC2 — all files clean (under caps + digest-only policy) → 0 violations, no notification\n#   TC3 — current-execution.md > 200 LOC → 1 violation\n#   TC4 — current-execution.md > 5 sessions → 1 violation\n#   TC5 — agent-notes.md > 700 LOC → 1 violation\n#   TC6 — agent-notes.md has inline ### L-S body → 1 violation (digest-only policy)\n#   TC7 — mistake-log.md has inline ### M-S body → 1 violation\n#   TC8 — multiple violations → notification lists all\n#   TC9 — telemetry > 10MB → 1 violation\n#   TC10 — empirical regression: grep returning rc=1 (0 matches) does NOT silent-exit\n#         (S100 PIPEFAIL fix verification — script reaches all 4 caps)\n#\n# Exit 0 = all pass. Exit 1 = any fail.\nset -uo pipefail\n\nSCRIPT_DIR=\"$(cd \"$(dirname \"${BASH_SOURCE[0]}\")\" && pwd)\"\nHOOK=\"$SCRIPT_DIR/../tracking-retention.sh\"\n[ ! -f \"$HOOK\" ] && { echo \"FAIL: hook script not found at $HOOK\"; exit 1; }\n\nTEMPDIR=$(mktemp -d)\ntrap '[ -n \"${KEEP_TEMP:-}\" ] && echo \"(KEEP_TEMP set; tempdir at $TEMPDIR)\" || rm -rf \"$TEMPDIR\"' EXIT\n\nMEM_DIR=\"$TEMPDIR/agent-workspace/memory\"\nNOTIF_DIR=\"$TEMPDIR/human-workspace/notifications\"\nLOG=\"$MEM_DIR/.session-hooks.log\"\nNOTIF_FILE=\"$NOTIF_DIR/tracking-retention-cap-breach.md\"\n\nclean_state() {\n  rm -rf \"$TEMPDIR/agent-workspace\" \"$TEMPDIR/human-workspace\"\n  mkdir -p \"$MEM_DIR\" \"$NOTIF_DIR\"\n}\n\nrun_hook() {\n  CLAUDE_PROJECT_DIR=\"$TEMPDIR\" bash \"$HOOK\" >/dev/null 2>&1\n}\n\nassert_violations_eq() {\n  local expected=\"$1\" tag=\"$2\"\n  local got\n  got=$(grep -oE \"violations=[0-9]+\" \"$LOG\" 2>/dev/null | tail -1 | sed 's/violations=//')\n  if [ \"${got:-MISSING}\" != \"$expected\" ]; then\n    echo \"FAIL $tag: expected violations=$expected; got violations=${got:-MISSING}\"\n    [ -f \"$LOG\" ] && cat \"$LOG\"\n    exit 1\n  fi\n}\n\n# --- TC1: all files missing → 0 violations, summary emitted, no notification ---\nclean_state\nrun_hook\nassert_violations_eq 0 \"TC1\"\nif [ -f \"$NOTIF_FILE\" ]; then\n  echo \"FAIL TC1: notification should NOT exist when 0 violations\"\n  exit 1\nfi\necho \"PASS TC1: missing files → 0 violations, no notification\"\n\n# --- TC2: clean state (small files, no inline bodies) → 0 violations ---\nclean_state\nprintf '# Header\\n\\n## S99 — short row\\n\\nClean.\\n' > \"$MEM_DIR/current-execution.md\"\nprintf '# Notes\\n\\nDigest table:\\n| L-S99-1 | digest |\\n' > \"$MEM_DIR/agent-notes.md\"\nprintf '# Mistakes\\n\\nDigest table:\\n| M-S99-1 | digest |\\n' > \"$MEM_DIR/mistake-log.md\"\n: > \"$MEM_DIR/component-telemetry.jsonl\"\nrun_hook\nassert_violations_eq 0 \"TC2\"\nif [ -f \"$NOTIF_FILE\" ]; then\n  echo \"FAIL TC2: notification should NOT exist when clean\"\n  exit 1\nfi\necho \"PASS TC2: clean state → 0 violations\"\n\n# --- TC3: current-execution.md > 200 LOC → 1 violation ---\nclean_state\n{\n  echo \"# Header\"\n  for i in $(seq 1 250); do echo \"line $i\"; done\n} > \"$MEM_DIR/current-execution.md\"\nrun_hook\nassert_violations_eq 1 \"TC3\"\nif ! grep -q \"current-execution.md LOC=\" \"$LOG\"; then\n  echo \"FAIL TC3: log should mention LOC violation\"\n  exit 1\nfi\necho \"PASS TC3: ce_loc > 200 → 1 violation\"\n\n# --- TC4: current-execution.md > 5 sessions → 1 violation ---\nclean_state\n{\n  echo \"# Header\"\n  for i in 1 2 3 4 5 6 7; do echo \"## S$i — row\"; done\n} > \"$MEM_DIR/current-execution.md\"\nrun_hook\nassert_violations_eq 1 \"TC4\"\nif ! grep -q \"ce_sessions=7\" \"$LOG\" 2>/dev/null; then\n  echo \"FAIL TC4: log should record ce_sessions=7\"\n  cat \"$LOG\"\n  exit 1\nfi\necho \"PASS TC4: ce_sessions > 5 → 1 violation\"\n\n# --- TC5: agent-notes.md > 700 LOC → 1 violation ---\nclean_state\n{ for i in $(seq 1 800); do echo \"line $i\"; done; } > \"$MEM_DIR/agent-notes.md\"\nrun_hook\nassert_violations_eq 1 \"TC5\"\nif ! grep -q \"agent-notes.md LOC=\" \"$LOG\"; then\n  echo \"FAIL TC5: log should mention agent-notes LOC violation\"\n  exit 1\nfi\necho \"PASS TC5: an_loc > 700 → 1 violation\"\n\n# --- TC6: agent-notes.md has inline ### L-S body → 1 violation ---\nclean_state\nprintf '# Notes\\n\\n### L-S99-1 — Inline body\\n\\nThis is a violation of digest-only policy.\\n' > \"$MEM_DIR/agent-notes.md\"\nrun_hook\nassert_violations_eq 1 \"TC6\"\nif ! grep -q \"an_lessons=1\" \"$LOG\"; then\n  echo \"FAIL TC6: log should record an_lessons=1\"\n  exit 1\nfi\necho \"PASS TC6: agent-notes has inline ### L-S → 1 violation\"\n\n# --- TC7: mistake-log.md has inline ### M-S body → 1 violation ---\nclean_state\nprintf '# Mistakes\\n\\n### M-S99-1 — Inline mistake body\\n\\nViolation of digest-only.\\n' > \"$MEM_DIR/mistake-log.md\"\nrun_hook\nassert_violations_eq 1 \"TC7\"\nif ! grep -q \"ml_mistakes=1\" \"$LOG\"; then\n  echo \"FAIL TC7: log should record ml_mistakes=1\"\n  exit 1\nfi\necho \"PASS TC7: mistake-log has inline ### M-S → 1 violation\"\n\n# --- TC8: multiple violations → notification lists all ---\nclean_state\n{ for i in $(seq 1 250); do echo \"line $i\"; done; } > \"$MEM_DIR/current-execution.md\"\n{ for i in $(seq 1 800); do echo \"line $i\"; done; } > \"$MEM_DIR/agent-notes.md\"\nprintf '# Mistakes\\n### M-S99-1 — body\\n' >> \"$MEM_DIR/agent-notes.md\"  # adds inline ### M-S to wrong file; harmless\nprintf '# Mistakes\\n### M-S99-1 — body\\n' > \"$MEM_DIR/mistake-log.md\"\nrun_hook\nNOTIF_COUNT=$(grep -c \"tracking-retention\" \"$NOTIF_FILE\" 2>/dev/null || true)\nNOTIF_COUNT=\"${NOTIF_COUNT:-0}\"\nif [ ! -f \"$NOTIF_FILE\" ]; then\n  echo \"FAIL TC8: notification file should exist\"\n  exit 1\nfi\nif [ \"$NOTIF_COUNT\" -lt 3 ]; then\n  echo \"FAIL TC8: notification should list ≥3 violation lines (got $NOTIF_COUNT)\"\n  cat \"$NOTIF_FILE\"\n  exit 1\nfi\necho \"PASS TC8: multiple violations listed in notification\"\n\n# --- TC9: telemetry > 10MB → 1 violation ---\nclean_state\n# Build 11MB file via dd-equivalent (POSIX yes/head); use head to capture exact size\nhead -c 11000000 /dev/zero > \"$MEM_DIR/component-telemetry.jsonl\" 2>/dev/null\nrun_hook\nassert_violations_eq 1 \"TC9\"\nif ! grep -q \"tel_bytes=\" \"$LOG\"; then\n  echo \"FAIL TC9: log should record tel_bytes\"\n  exit 1\nfi\necho \"PASS TC9: telemetry > 10MB → 1 violation\"\n\n# --- TC10: PIPEFAIL regression — grep -c returning rc=1 (0 matches) does NOT silent-exit ---\n# Setup state where ALL grep -c invocations return 0 matches (rc=1 each):\n# - current-execution.md: NO `^## S[0-9]` lines + LOC 5 (under 200)\n# - agent-notes.md: NO `^### L-S` lines + LOC 5 (under 700)\n# - mistake-log.md: NO `^### M-S` lines + LOC 5 (under 200)\n# - component-telemetry.jsonl: tiny\n# Pre-S100 fix: script silent-exits at first grep -c (line 43 ce_sessions) before reaching telemetry.\n# Post-S100 fix: script reaches all 4 caps, summary line shows all 4 metrics.\nclean_state\nprintf '# Header\\nNo session rows.\\n' > \"$MEM_DIR/current-execution.md\"\nprintf '# Notes\\nDigest only.\\n' > \"$MEM_DIR/agent-notes.md\"\nprintf '# Mistakes\\nDigest only.\\n' > \"$MEM_DIR/mistake-log.md\"\n: > \"$MEM_DIR/component-telemetry.jsonl\"\nrun_hook\nSUMMARY=$(grep \"tracking-retention: violations=\" \"$LOG\" 2>/dev/null | tail -1)\nif [ -z \"$SUMMARY\" ]; Exit 1 = any fail.\nset -uo pipefail\n\nSCRIPT_DIR=\"$(cd \"$(dirname \"${BASH_SOURCE[0]}\")\" && pwd)\"\nHOOK=\"$SCRIPT_DIR/../tracking-retention.sh\"\n[ ! -f \"$HOOK\" ] && { echo \"FAIL: hook script not found at $HOOK\"; then\n    echo \"FAIL $tag: expected violations=$expected; got violations=${got:-MISSING}\"\n    [ -f \"$LOG\" ] && cat \"$LOG\"\n    exit 1\n  fi\n}\n\n# --- TC1: all files missing → 0 violations, summary emitted, no notification ---\nclean_state\nrun_hook\nassert_violations_eq 0 \"TC1\"\nif [ -f \"$NOTIF_FILE\" ]; do echo \"line $i\"; done; } > \"$MEM_DIR/current-execution.md\"\n{ for i in $(seq 1 800); do echo \"line $i\"; done; } > \"$MEM_DIR/agent-notes.md\"\nprintf '# Mistakes\\n### M-S99-1 — body\\n' >> \"$MEM_DIR/agent-notes.md\"  # adds inline ### M-S to wrong file; harmless\nprintf '# Mistakes\\n### M-S99-1 — body\\n' > \"$MEM_DIR/mistake-log.md\"\nrun_hook\nNOTIF_COUNT=$(grep -c \"tracking-retention\" \"$NOTIF_FILE\" 2>/dev/null || true)\nNOTIF_COUNT=\"${NOTIF_COUNT:-0}\"\nif [ ! -f \"$NOTIF_FILE\" ]; then\n  echo \"FAIL TC8: notification file should exist\"\n  exit 1\nfi\nif [ \"$NOTIF_COUNT\" -lt 3 ]; then\n  echo \"FAIL TC8: notification should list ≥3 violation lines (got $NOTIF_COUNT)\"\n  cat \"$NOTIF_FILE\"\n  exit 1\nfi\necho \"PASS TC8: multiple violations listed in notification\"\n\n# --- TC9: telemetry > 10MB → 1 violation ---\nclean_state\n# Build 11MB file via dd-equivalent (POSIX yes/head);
